----- [assistant] -----
# 🤖 EA Design: Deep Learning Indicators — Workflow & Concept

จากไฟล์ที่คุณมี (10 AI Trading Systems ที่ใช้ GRU Neural Network) [1] ผมจะออกแบบ EA เป็น **3 ระบบหลัก** โดยจับคู่ Indicator ให้เสริมกัน ดังนี้

---

## 🏗️ Architecture Overview

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              MASTER EA CONTROLLER                   │
│         (Signal Aggregator + Risk Manager)          │
└──────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬────────────┘
           │              │              │
     SYSTEM A       SYSTEM B       SYSTEM C
   TREND MODE     MOMENTUM MODE  VOLATILITY MODE
```

---

## ⚙️ SYSTEM A — "Trend Confirmation Engine"

> **เหมาะกับ:** EUR/USD, GBP/USD, USD/JPY | Timeframe: **H1 / H4**

### 🔗 Indicator Combo
| Layer | Indicator | บทบาท |
|---|---|---|
| 1st Filter | **ADX Deep Learning** | กรองว่าตลาดมีเทรนด์จริงหรือไม่ |
| 2nd Filter | **Alligator Deep Learning** | ระบุทิศทางเทรนด์ (Jaw/Teeth/Lips) |
| 3rd Confirm | **Parabolic SAR Deep Learning** | Confirm Entry Point + Reversal |

### 📐 Logic Flow
```
[ADX Signal]
   ├─ ADX > 25 (มีเทรนด์) → ผ่านไป Layer 2
   └─ ADX < 20 (Sideway) → SKIP ❌

[Alligator Signal]
   ├─ BUY: Lips > Teeth > Jaw (เส้นแยกขึ้น) → ผ่านไป Layer 3
   └─ SELL: Lips < Teeth < Jaw (เส้นแยกลง) → ผ่านไป Layer 3

[Parabolic SAR Signal]
   ├─ SAR อยู่ใต้ราคา → CONFIRM BUY ✅
   └─ SAR อยู่เหนือราคา → CONFIRM SELL ✅

→ ENTRY เมื่อ ทั้ง 3 Layer ตรงกัน
```

### ⚙️ Parameter Settings
```
ADX Period     : 14  | Threshold: 25
Alligator Jaw  : 13  | Teeth: 8 | Lips: 5
SAR Step       : 0.02 | Maximum: 0.2
GRU Hidden     : 6 units (ทุกตัว)
```

---

## ⚙️ SYSTEM B — "Momentum Reversal Engine"

> **เหมาะกับ:** XAU/USD (Gold), GBP/JPY | Timeframe: **M15 / M30**

### 🔗 Indicator Combo
| Layer | Indicator | บทบาท |
|---|---|---|
| 1st Filter | **RSI Deep Learning** | จับ Overbought/Oversold |
| 2nd Filter | **MACD Deep Learning** | ยืนยัน Momentum + Crossover |
| 3rd Confirm | **Bull Bear Power Deep Learning** | ดูว่า Bulls หรือ Bears คุม |
| 4th Confirm | **RVI Deep Learning** | Cross Zero-line ยืนยัน Vigor |

### 📐 Logic Flow
```
[RSI Signal]
   ├─ RSI < 30 (Oversold) → Watch for BUY
   └─ RSI > 70 (Overbought) → Watch for SELL

[MACD Signal]
   ├─ MACD Line ตัด Signal Line ขึ้น → BUY Momentum ✅
   └─ MACD Line ตัด Signal Line ลง → SELL Momentum ✅

[Bull Bear Power]
   ├─ Bull Power > 0 → Bulls ควบคุม → สนับสนุน BUY
   └─ Bear Power < 0 → Bears ควบคุม → สนับสนุน SELL

[RVI Confirm]
   ├─ RVI Cross Signal ขึ้น + Zero-line → FINAL BUY ✅
   └─ RVI Cross Signal ลง + Zero-line → FINAL SELL ✅

→ ENTRY เมื่อ 3 ใน 4 Layer ตรงกัน (Voting System)
```

### ⚙️ Parameter Settings
```
RSI Period     : 14  | OB: 70 | OS: 30
MACD Fast      : 12  | Slow: 26 | Signal: 9
Bull EMA       : 13  | Bull Threshold: 0.001
Bear Threshold : -0.001
RVI Period     : 10  | Signal Period: 4
GRU Hidden     : 6 units (ทุกตัว)
```

---

## ⚙️ SYSTEM C — "Volatility Breakout Engine"

> **เหมาะกับ:** USD/JPY, EUR/GBP, NAS100 | Timeframe: **M30 / H1**

### 🔗 Indicator Combo
| Layer | Indicator | บทบาท |
|---|---|---|
| 1st Filter | **ATR Deep Learning** | วัดความผันผวน + Breakout Zone |
| 2nd Filter | **Envelope Deep Learning** | กำหนด Dynamic Support/Resistance |
| 3rd Confirm | **Moving Average Deep Learning** | ยืนยันทิศทาง Mean Reversion |

### 📐 Logic Flow
```
[ATR Signal]
   ├─ ATR สูงกว่าค่าเฉลี่ย 20 แท่ง → Volatility สูง → พร้อม Breakout
   └─ ATR ต่ำมาก → SKIP ❌

[Envelope Signal]
   ├─ ราคา Break Upper Band → SELL (Mean Reversion) หรือ BUY (Breakout)
   └─ ราคา Break Lower Band → BUY (Mean Reversion) หรือ SELL (Breakout)

[MA Deep Learning]
   ├─ ราคา > MA + GRU BUY Signal → CONFIRM BUY ✅
   └─ ราคา < MA + GRU SELL Signal → CONFIRM SELL ✅

→ ENTRY เมื่อ ATR + Envelope + MA ตรงกัน
```

### ⚙️ Parameter Settings
```
ATR Period     : 14  | Multiplier: 2.0
MA Period      : 20  | Multiplier: 1.5
Envelope MA    : 20  | Deviation: 0.5%
GRU Hidden     : 6 units (ทุกตัว)
```

---

## 🛡️ Risk Management Layer (ทุก System ใช้ร่วมกัน)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│         RISK MANAGEMENT MODULE          │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Position Sizing  : 1-2% Risk per Trade  │
│ Stop Loss        : ATR × 1.5 (Dynamic)  │
│ Take Profit      : Risk:Reward = 1:2    │
│ Max DD Control   : หยุดเมื่อ DD > 10%  │
│ Max Open Trades  : 3 Positions          │
│ Force Signal     : หลัง 15 แท่งไม่มี   │
│                    สัญญาณ (NERVOUSCORE) │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 🗺️ Master Workflow Diagram

```
Market Data (Tick/Bar)
        │
        ▼
┌───────────────────┐
│  Timeframe Filter │ ← เลือก TF ตาม System
└────────┬──────────┘
         │
         ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────┐
│     GRU Signal Collection Layer    │
│  [ADX][Alligator][SAR][RSI][MACD]  │
│  [BBP][RVI][ATR][ENV][MA] → 0/1    │
└────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────┐
│       Signal Voting Engine         │
│   System A: AND Logic (3/3)        │
│   System B: Majority Vote (3/4)    │
│   System C: AND Logic (3/3)        │
└────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────┐
│      Risk Management Filter        │
│  DD Check → Sizing → SL/TP Calc    │
└────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
          ORDER EXECUTION
         (MT5 Trade Module)
                 │
                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────┐
│    Performance Tracking (Real-time)│
│  Accuracy | WinRate | Confidence   │
└────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 📋 สรุป EA Matrix

| System | Indicators | คู่เงินแนะนำ | TF | สไตล์ |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **A: Trend** | ADX + Alligator + SAR | EUR/USD, GBP/USD | H1/H4 | Trend Following |
| **B: Momentum** | RSI + MACD + BBP + RVI | XAU/USD, GBP/JPY | M15/M30 | Reversal |
| **C: Volatility** | ATR + Envelope + MA | USD/JPY, NAS100 | M30/H1 | Breakout |

# 🎯 System A — FxDreema EA Design

## "Trend Confirmation Engine" | ADX + Alligator + Parabolic SAR

---

## 📌 FxDreema คืออะไรก่อน (Context)

FxDreema ใช้ **Visual Block-based Programming** แทน Code
แต่ละ Block คือ Logic Node ที่เชื่อมกันด้วย **Condition Arrows**

---

## 🗺️ Full Workflow Diagram (System A)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    NEW BAR EVENT (H1)                       │
│                   [OnNewBar Trigger]                        │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│               BLOCK 1: TRADE FILTER ZONE                    │
│  ┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────┐   │
│  │ Max Open Trades  │    │   Trading Hours Filter       │   │
│  │   Check ≤ 1      │    │   08:00 - 20:00 (GMT+2)      │   │
│  └────────┬─────────┘    └──────────────┬───────────────┘   │
│           └──────────────┬──────────────┘                   │
│                       PASS ✅                               │
└──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                           │
                           ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           BLOCK 2: ADX DEEP LEARNING SIGNAL                 │
│                                                             │
│   ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐                   │
│   │  ADX Indicator Read                 │                   │
│   │  Period = 14                        │                   │
│   │  Threshold = 25                     │                   │
│   └──────────────┬──────────────────────┘                   │
│                  │                                          │
│        ┌─────────▼──────────┐                               │
│        │  ADX Value > 25 ?  │                               │
│        └──┬──────────────┬──┘                               │
│         YES              NO                                 │
│          │                └──→ [SKIP - No Trade] ❌          │
│          ▼                                                  │
│   ┌──────────────────────────────┐                          │
│   │  Read DI+ และ DI-            │                          │
│   │  GRU Signal = BUY or SELL ?  │                          │
│   └──────────────┬───────────────┘                          │
│                  │                                          │
│         ┌────────▼────────┐                                 │
│         │ DI+ > DI- ?     │                                 │
│         └──┬───────────┬──┘                                 │
│          BUY           SELL                                 │
│          │               │                                  │
│    [Flag=BUY]      [Flag=SELL]                              │
└──────────┬───────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘
           │               │
           ▼               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│         BLOCK 3: ALLIGATOR DEEP LEARNING SIGNAL             │
│                                                             │
│   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐               │
│   │  Alligator Read                         │               │
│   │  Jaw=13 | Teeth=8 | Lips=5             │               │
│   └──────────────────┬──────────────────────┘               │
│                      │                                      │
│         ┌────────────▼────────────┐                         │
│         │  BUY Path:              │                         │
│         │  Lips > Teeth > Jaw ?   │                         │
│         │  (เส้นแยกออกขึ้น)       │                         │
│         └────────┬────────────────┘                         │
│                YES → Flag ยังเป็น BUY ✅                    │
│                NO  → [CANCEL Signal] ❌                     │
│                                                             │
│         ┌────────────▼────────────┐                         │
│         │  SELL Path:             │                         │
│         │  Lips < Teeth < Jaw ?   │                         │
│         │  (เส้นแยกออกลง)         │                         │
│         └────────┬────────────────┘                         │
│                YES → Flag ยังเป็น SELL ✅                   │
│                NO  → [CANCEL Signal] ❌                     │
└──────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│        BLOCK 4: PARABOLIC SAR DEEP LEARNING SIGNAL          │
│                                                             │
│   ┌──────────────────────────────────────┐                  │
│   │  Parabolic SAR Read                  │                  │
│   │  Step=0.02 | Maximum=0.2             │                  │
│   └──────────────────┬───────────────────┘                  │
│                      │                                      │
│         ┌────────────▼────────────┐                         │
│         │  BUY Path:              │                         │
│         │  SAR < Current Price ?  │                         │
│         │  (SAR อยู่ใต้แท่งเทียน) │                         │
│         └────────┬────────────────┘                         │
│                YES → FINAL CONFIRM BUY ✅✅✅               │
│                NO  → [CANCEL] ❌                            │
│                                                             │
│         ┌────────────▼────────────┐                         │
│         │  SELL Path:             │                         │
│         │  SAR > Current Price ?  │                         │
│         │  (SAR อยู่เหนือแท่งเทียน)│                        │
│         └────────┬────────────────┘                         │
│                YES → FINAL CONFIRM SELL ✅✅✅              │
│                NO  → [CANCEL] ❌                            │
└──────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            BLOCK 5: RISK MANAGEMENT CALCULATOR              │
│                                                             │
│   ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐       │
│   │  ATR(14) Read → คำนวณ Dynamic SL/TP             │       │
│   │  Stop Loss   = ATR × 1.5                        │       │
│   │  Take Profit = ATR × 3.0  (RR = 1:2)           │       │
│   │  Lot Size    = (Balance × 1%) ÷ SL_pips        │       │
│   └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘       │
└──────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              BLOCK 6: ORDER EXECUTION                       │
│                                                             │
│   ┌──────────────────────┐  ┌───────────────────────────┐   │
│   │   OPEN BUY ORDER     │  │   OPEN SELL ORDER         │   │
│   │  - Market Order      │  │  - Market Order           │   │
│   │  - SL = Price - ATR×1.5│ │  - SL = Price + ATR×1.5  │  │
│   │  - TP = Price + ATR×3 │  │  - TP = Price - ATR×3    │   │
│   │  - Lot = Calculated   │  │  - Lot = Calculated       │   │
│   └──────────────────────┘  └───────────────────────────┘   │
└──────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│           BLOCK 7: TRADE MANAGEMENT (Trailing)              │
│                                                             │
│   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐      │
│   │  Profit > ATR×1.5 ?                              │      │
│   │  YES → Move SL to Breakeven                      │      │
│   │  Profit > ATR×2.5 ?                              │      │
│   │  YES → Trailing Stop = ATR×1.0                   │      │
│   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 🧱 FxDreema Block List (ที่ต้องใช้จริง)

| # | Block Name ใน FxDreema | ตั้งค่า | หน้าที่ |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **New Bar** | H1 | Trigger ทุกแท่งใหม่ |
| 2 | **Indicator: ADX** | Period=14 | อ่านค่า ADX |
| 3 | **Condition: Value >** | 25 | กรองเทรนด์ |
| 4 | **Indicator: Alligator** | 13/8/5 | อ่าน Jaw/Teeth/Lips |
| 5 | **Condition: Value >** | Lips>Teeth>Jaw | ตรวจทิศทาง |
| 6 | **Indicator: Parabolic SAR** | 0.02/0.2 | อ่าน SAR |
| 7 | **Condition: SAR < Price** | — | Confirm Entry |
| 8 | **Indicator: ATR** | Period=14 | คำนวณ SL/TP |
| 9 | **Math Operation** | ATR×1.5 / ATR×3 | คำนวณระยะ |
| 10 | **Position Size Calc** | Risk=1% | คำนวณ Lot |
| 11 | **Open Order (Buy/Sell)** | Market | เปิด Order |
| 12 | **Modify Order** | Breakeven Logic | ย้าย SL |
| 13 | **Trailing Stop** | ATR×1.0 | Trailing |
| 14 | **Count Open Trades** | ≤1 | จำกัด Trade |
| 15 | **Time Filter** | 08:00-20:00 | กรอง Session |

---

## 🔗 FxDreema Connection Flow (ภาพรวม Block)

```
[New Bar H1]
      │
      ├──→ [Time Filter 08-20] ──FAIL──→ [Stop]
      │           │PASS
      ├──→ [Count Trades ≤ 1] ──FAIL──→ [Stop]
      │           │PASS
      ├──→ [Read ADX(14)]
      │           │
      ├──→ [ADX > 25?] ──NO──→ [Stop]
      │           │YES
      ├──→ [Read DI+ vs DI-]
      │      ┌────┴────┐
      │    DI+>DI-   DI->DI+
      │      │BUY      │SELL
      ├──→ [Read Alligator 13/8/5]
      │      ┌────────────────────┐
      │   Lips>Teeth>Jaw?    Lips<Teeth<Jaw?
      │      │YES                  │YES
      ├──→ [Read SAR 0.02/0.2]
      │      ┌──────────────────────┐
      │   SAR<Price?           SAR>Price?
      │      │YES                    │YES
      ├──→ [Read ATR(14)]
      │           │
      ├──→ [Calc SL = ATR×1.5]
      ├──→ [Calc TP = ATR×3.0]
      ├──→ [Calc Lot = 1% Risk]
      │           │
      ├──→ [Open BUY]          [Open SELL]
      │           │
      └──→ [Monitor: Breakeven → Trailing]
```

---

## ⚙️ Input Parameters Summary (ตั้งใน FxDreema)

```
═══════════════════════════════════════
  SYSTEM A - TREND CONFIRMATION EA
  Pair    : EURUSD
  TF      : H1
═══════════════════════════════════════
  ADX Period        : 14
  ADX Threshold     : 25
─────────────────────────────────────
  Alligator Jaw     : 13 (shift 8)
  Alligator Teeth   : 8  (shift 5)
  Alligator Lips    : 5  (shift 3)
─────────────────────────────────────
  SAR Step          : 0.02
  SAR Maximum       : 0.2
─────────────────────────────────────
  ATR Period        : 14
  SL Multiplier     : 1.5
  TP Multiplier     : 3.0
─────────────────────────────────────
  Risk Per Trade    : 1%
  Max Open Trades   : 1
  Trading Hours     : 08:00 - 20:00
═══════════════════════════════════════
```

---

## 🎯 สรุป Decision Tree แบบง่าย

```
ADX > 25 ✅
    + DI+ > DI- (BUY) หรือ DI- > DI+ (SELL) ✅
        + Alligator เส้นแยก (ทิศทางเดียวกัน) ✅
            + SAR อยู่ฝั่งที่ถูกต้อง ✅
                → FIRE ORDER 🚀
```

```html
<!-- ===================== SYSTEM A ===================== -->
<div id="tab-a" class="tab-content active">
  <div class="sys-title color-a">⚡ SYSTEM A — TREND CONFIRMATION ENGINE</div>
  <div class="sys-subtitle">ADX Deep Learning + Alligator Deep Learning + Parabolic SAR Deep Learning | EUR/USD, GBP/USD | H1 / H4</div>

  <div class="grid-2">
    <!-- Workflow -->
    <div class="card card-a" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">
      <h3 class="a">🗺️ FXDREEMA BLOCK WORKFLOW</h3>
      <div class="flow-wrap">

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">0</span><span class="flow-title a">TRIGGER — New Bar (H1/H4)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">OnNewBar Event → เริ่มรันทุกแท่งเทียนใหม่ | ตั้งค่า: Symbol=EURUSD, TF=H1</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">1</span><span class="flow-title a">FILTER — Time & Trade Count</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            🕐 Time Filter: 08:00–20:00 GMT+2 → FAIL = Stop<br>
            📊 Count Open Trades ≤ 1 → FAIL = Stop
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">2</span><span class="flow-title a">LAYER 1 — ADX Deep Learning Signal</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read ADX(14) value<br>
            ✅ Condition: ADX &gt; 25 → มีเทรนด์จริง (ถ้า &lt;25 → SKIP)<br>
            📥 Read DI+ และ DI- → DI+ &gt; DI- = BUY flag | DI- &gt; DI+ = SELL flag<br>
            🤖 GRU Signal Confirm: BUY(1) / SELL(0)
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">3</span><span class="flow-title a">LAYER 2 — Alligator Deep Learning Signal</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read Jaw(13), Teeth(8), Lips(5)<br>
            ✅ BUY: Lips &gt; Teeth &gt; Jaw (เส้นแยกขึ้น — ตลาดตื่น)<br>
            ✅ SELL: Lips &lt; Teeth &lt; Jaw (เส้นแยกลง — ตลาดตื่น)<br>
            ❌ เส้นพันกัน = Alligator หลับ → CANCEL Signal
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">4</span><span class="flow-title a">LAYER 3 — Parabolic SAR Deep Learning Confirm</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read SAR(Step=0.02, Max=0.2)<br>
            ✅ BUY: SAR &lt; Current Price (SAR อยู่ใต้แท่ง)<br>
            ✅ SELL: SAR &gt; Current Price (SAR อยู่เหนือแท่ง)<br>
            🤖 GRU Reversal Confirm → ยืนยัน Entry Point สุดท้าย
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="fork-row">
          <div class="fork-block fork-a-buy">
            <div class="fork-label">✅ CONFIRM BUY</div>
            <div class="fork-sub">ADX&gt;25 + DI+&gt;DI- + Lips&gt;Teeth&gt;Jaw + SAR&lt;Price</div>
          </div>
          <div class="fork-block fork-a-sell">
            <div class="fork-label">✅ CONFIRM SELL</div>
            <div class="fork-sub">ADX&gt;25 + DI-&gt;DI+ + Lips&lt;Teeth&lt;Jaw + SAR&gt;Price</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">5</span><span class="flow-title a">RISK CALC — ATR-Based SL/TP/Lot</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read ATR(14)<br>
            🔴 Stop Loss = ATR × 1.5 (Dynamic)<br>
            🟢 Take Profit = ATR × 3.0 → RR = 1:2<br>
            📦 Lot Size = (Balance × 1%) ÷ SL_pips
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">6</span><span class="flow-title a">EXECUTION — Open Market Order</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">Open BUY / SELL → Market Order | SL, TP, Lot ตามที่คำนวณ | Magic Number = 1001</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-a"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-a">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-a">7</span><span class="flow-title a">MANAGEMENT — Breakeven + Trailing Stop</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📈 Profit &gt; ATR×1.5 → Move SL to Breakeven<br>
            📈 Profit &gt; ATR×2.5 → Activate Trailing Stop (ATR×1.0)<br>
            🛑 Drawdown &gt; 10% → Stop Trading (DD Guard)
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read RSI(14)<br>
            🔴 RSI &gt; 70 → Overbought → Watch SELL Zone<br>
            🟢 RSI &lt; 30 → Oversold → Watch BUY Zone<br>
            🤖 GRU Divergence Detect → เพิ่มความแม่นยำ Reversal<br>
            ⚠️ RSI 30–70 → HOLD (ไม่เข้า)
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-b"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-b">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-b">3</span><span class="flow-title b">LAYER 2 — MACD Deep Learning (Momentum Confirm)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read MACD(12,26,9): Main, Signal, Histogram<br>
            ✅ BUY: MACD Line ตัด Signal Line ขึ้น + Histogram เปลี่ยนเป็นบวก<br>
            ✅ SELL: MACD Line ตัด Signal Line ลง + Histogram เปลี่ยนเป็นลบ<br>
            🤖 GRU Crossover Confirm → กรอง False Crossover
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-b"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-b">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-b">4</span><span class="flow-title b">LAYER 3 — Bull Bear Power (Market Control)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read Bull Power และ Bear Power (EMA=13)<br>
            🐂 BUY Path: Bull Power &gt; 0.001 → Bulls ควบคุม<br>
            🐻 SELL Path: Bear Power &lt; -0.001 → Bears ควบคุม<br>
            🤖 GRU Momentum Confirm → Validate ผู้ควบคุมตลาด
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-b"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-b">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-b">5</span><span class="flow-title b">LAYER 4 — RVI Deep Learning (Final Vigor Confirm)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read RVI(10) และ Signal Line(4)<br>
            ✅ BUY: RVI ตัด Signal ขึ้น + Cross Zero-line<br>
            ✅ SELL: RVI ตัด Signal ลง + Cross Zero-line<br>
            🤖 GRU Vigor Gate → Final confirmation ก่อน Entry
          </div>
        </div>

        </div>

          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-b"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-b">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-b">6</span><span class="flow-title b">RISK CALC — Fixed SL + RR Based TP</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read ATR(14) → Dynamic Risk<br>
            🔴 Stop Loss = ATR × 1.2 (Tight — Reversal System)<br>
            🟢 Take Profit = ATR × 2.4 → RR = 1:2<br>
            📦 Lot Size = (Balance × 1.5%) ÷ SL_pips (Risk เพิ่มเล็กน้อยเพราะ Reversal)
          </div>
        </div>

        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-b"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-b">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-b">8</span><span class="flow-title b">MANAGEMENT — Partial Close + Trailing</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📈 Profit @ RR 1:1 → Close 50% Position (Partial Close)<br>
            📈 Profit &gt; ATR×2.0 → Trailing Stop = ATR×0.8<br>
            🔄 RSI กลับสู่ 50 Zone → Force Close ส่วนที่เหลือ
          </div>
        </div>

<!-- ===================== SYSTEM C ===================== -->
<div id="tab-c" class="tab-content">
  <div class="sys-title color-c">💥 SYSTEM C — VOLATILITY BREAKOUT ENGINE</div>
  <div class="sys-subtitle">ATR + Envelope + Moving Average Deep Learning | USD/JPY, EUR/GBP, NAS100 | M30 / H1</div>

  <div class="grid-2">
    <div class="card card-c" style="grid-column: 1 / -1;">
      <h3 class="c">🗺️ FXDREEMA BLOCK WORKFLOW</h3>
      <div class="flow-wrap">

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">0</span><span class="flow-title c">TRIGGER — New Bar (M30/H1)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">OnNewBar Event | Symbol=USDJPY / EURJPY / NAS100 | TF=H1</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">1</span><span class="flow-title c">FILTER — Volatility Pre-Check</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📊 Read ATR(14) → Compare กับ ATR MA(20)<br>
            ✅ ATR &gt; ATR_MA × 1.2 → Volatility สูงพอ → ผ่าน<br>
            ❌ ATR ต่ำเกินไป = ตลาดนิ่ง → SKIP ทั้งหมด<br>
            📊 Count Open Trades ≤ 2
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">2</span><span class="flow-title c">LAYER 1 — ATR Deep Learning (Volatility Zone)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read ATR(14) + Upper/Lower Band<br>
            🔥 ATR Band Upper = MA + ATR×2.0<br>
            🔥 ATR Band Lower = MA - ATR×2.0<br>
            ✅ ราคา Break Upper → สัญญาณ Breakout UP<br>
            ✅ ราคา Break Lower → สัญญาณ Breakout DOWN<br>
            🤖 GRU Volatility Confirm → ยืนยันว่าเป็น Real Break ไม่ใช่ Fake
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">3</span><span class="flow-title c">LAYER 2 — Envelope Deep Learning (Dynamic S/R)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read Envelope(MA=20, Dev=0.5%)<br>
            ✅ Breakout Mode: ราคา Close เหนือ Upper Band → BUY<br>
            ✅ Breakout Mode: ราคา Close ต่ำกว่า Lower Band → SELL<br>
            ✅ Mean Reversion Mode: ราคากลับเข้า Band → Counter Trade<br>
            🤖 GRU Band Strength → วัดความแข็งแกร่งของ Band Breakout
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">4</span><span class="flow-title c">LAYER 3 — Moving Average Deep Learning (Trend Direction)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 Read MA(20) + Dynamic Bands (Multiplier=1.5)<br>
            ✅ BUY: ราคา &gt; MA + GRU BUY Signal = ทิศทางขึ้น<br>
            ✅ SELL: ราคา &lt; MA + GRU SELL Signal = ทิศทางลง<br>
            🤖 GRU Crossover Detect → Momentum + Trend Strength<br>
            ⚠️ ราคาในช่วง MA Band → HOLD
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div style="font-size:0.75rem; color:#bf5fff; text-align:center; padding:6px 0; font-weight:700;">
          🔀 MODE SELECTOR: Breakout Mode vs Mean Reversion Mode
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="fork-row">
          <div class="fork-block fork-c-bo">
            <div class="fork-label">💥 BREAKOUT MODE</div>
            <div class="fork-sub">ATR High + Price Break Band + MA Confirm Direction</div>
          </div>
          <div class="fork-block fork-c-mr">
            <div class="fork-label">↩️ MEAN REVERSION MODE</div>
            <div class="fork-sub">Price Overextended + Return to MA + Band Contract</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">5</span><span class="flow-title c">RISK CALC — Wider SL for Volatility</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            📥 ATR(14) → Wider SL เพราะ Volatility สูง<br>
            🔴 Breakout SL = ATR × 2.0 (ใต้/เหนือ Band)<br>
            🟢 Breakout TP = ATR × 4.0 → RR = 1:2<br>
            🔴 Mean Rev SL = ATR × 1.0 (แคบกว่า)<br>
            🟢 Mean Rev TP = ATR × 2.0<br>
            📦 Lot Size = (Balance × 1%) ÷ SL_pips
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">6</span><span class="flow-title c">EXECUTION — Open Market Order</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">Open BUY / SELL → Market Order | Magic Number = 3003</div>
        </div>
        <div class="flow-arrow arrow-c"></div>

        <div class="flow-block flow-block-c">
          <div class="flow-header"><span class="flow-num fn-c">7</span><span class="flow-title c">MANAGEMENT — Aggressive Trailing (Breakout)</span></div>
          <div class="flow-desc">
            💥 Breakout: Profit &gt; ATR×2.0 → Trailing ATR×1.5 (ให้ Run)<br>
            ↩️ Mean Rev: Profit &gt; ATR×1.0 → Close @ Target (ไม่ Trailing)<br>
            ⏰ Max Hold Time: 48 แท่ง → Force Close ถ้าไม่ถึง TP<br>
            🛑 Drawdown Guard: DD &gt; 8% → Stop System
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

<script>
  function switchTab(id, btn) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
      b.classList.remove('active-a','active-b','active-c');
      b.style.color = '';
    });
    document.getElementById('tab-' + id).classList.add('active');
    if(id === 'a') btn.classList.add('active-a');
    else if(id === 'b') btn.classList.add('active-b');
    else if(id === 'c') btn.classList.add('active-c');
    else { btn.style.color = '#e0e6f0'; btn.style.borderBottomColor = '#e0e6f0'; btn.style.background = '#1e2a3a'; }
  }

  let resizeTimer;
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
    resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => {}, 150);
  });
</script>
```

| Tab | เนื้อหา |
|---|---|
| **⚡ System A** | Trend Confirmation — ADX + Alligator + SAR + Block List 16 blocks |
| **🔥 System B** | Momentum Reversal — RSI + MACD + BBP + RVI + Voting Logic 20 blocks |
| **💥 System C** | Volatility Breakout — ATR + Envelope + MA + Dual Mode 18 blocks |
| **📋 Summary** | ตารางเปรียบเทียบ + Deployment Order |

Money management, stop-loss, take-profit and other options are available.\\nHow It Works?\\n1. Parameters for the trade are calculated given the settings you provide.\\n2. Parameters are then checked and corrected if needed.\\n3. A request for a new trade is sent to your broker&#x27;s server.\\n4. If a new trade has been created successfully, the output becomes active. If not, the behavior depends on the error response from the server. Either a new trade attempt is initiated or the . output becomes active, signaling for a fatal error.\\n- If the requested lot size is lower than the minimum available, the minimum is taken. If it is greater than the maximum - the maximum is taken.\\nNote that lot sizes are always rounded, because there is a minimum lot step required. For example, if the lot step is 0.01, then a lot size of 0.1 or 0.11 is valid, but 0.102 is not valid and it will be automatically corrected to the nearest valid, which is 0.10.\\n- Open price is Ask price.\\n- Stop-loss and take-profit are calculated in relation to Ask price!\\n- If stop-loss or take-profit is too short, the minimum level is taken.\\nActivate Windows Go to Settings to activate Windows.\\nCounters\\nMore ...\\nBuy now\\nGroup and Symbol to use\\nGroup # Symbol\\nMoney management\\nMoney management How much ...\\nFixed volume\\n0.1\\nlots\\nVolume upper limit\\nlots\\nBuy pending order Buy pending orders in grid Sell now\\nSell pending order\\nSell pending orders in grid\\nBucket of Trades &amp; Orders\\nLoop for Trades &amp; Orders\\nTrailing Stop / Break Even\\nTrading Actions\\nChart &amp; Objects\\nLoop for Chart Objects\\nOutput &amp; Communication\\nVarious Signals\\nControlling Blocks\\nFlags\\nProfile\\nOptions\\nHelp\\nTutorial Examples Forum\\n263 Full\\nHistory\\nX\\nQ\\n...\\nhttps://fxdreema.com/builder\\n&gt; C Projects\\nMy Indicators\\nBuy now\\nVariables\\nJOBOT\\nx\\n+\\nfxdreema\\n.mq\\ncex4\\nEvents\\non Init\\non Timer\\non Tick 1\\non Trade\\non Chart\\non Deinit\\nJOBOT\\n1 Constants (Inputs)\\n1 Variables\\nSystem\\nCustom\\nSearch:\\nVariables\\nCondition &amp; Formula\\nGroup #\\nSymbol\\nMoney management\\nMoney management\\nFixed volume\\n0.1\\nlots\\nVolume upper limit\\nlots\\nStop-Loss and Take-Profit\\nStop-Loss mode\\nFixed pips\\nin pips ...\\n50 pips\\nTake-Profit mode\\nFixed pips\\nin pips ...\\n50\\npips\\nExpiration (works while the robot is running)\\nExpiration mode\\nNo expiration\\nMore settings\\nSlippage\\n4 pips\\nComment\\ntext\\nArrow Color\\nRed\\nChange Status to\\nUpdate\\nDelete\\nBlock Information\\nx\\n1. Money management, stop-loss, take-profit, expiration, gradation and other options are available.\\nGradation means that the given parameter (for example stop-loss size) for the current pending order is X% from the same parameter for the previous pending order in the grid. The default gradation value for all parameters is 100%, which means that for each pending order parameters are equal.\\nFor example, If you select gradation of 200% - every next pending order will have this parameter 2 times bigger than the same parameter for the previous pending order in the grid.\\nHow It Works?\\n1. Parameters for the first pending order are calculated given the settings you provide.\\n2. Parameters are then checked and corrected if needed.\\n3. The first pending order request is sent to the server.\\n4. If there is an error, a new attempt is initiated. If the error is fatal this pending order is abandoned.\\n5. If the pending order is placed successfully then 1-4 is repeated for the next pending order in the grid, until all pending orders are placed (if no fatal errors).\\n- If the requested lot size is lower than the minimum available, the minimum is taken. Use \\\&quot;For each Pending Order\\\&quot; block somewhere above this block.\\nIf you are sliding relative to the current Open Price, you will see that the pending order is moving in the same direction every time the block runs. This speed of movement depends mostly on how often the block runs. If the block runs too often, the pending order looks like it tries to escape outside the chart or to crash into the market price.\\nIf you are sliding relative to the current market price, the pending order is kept at certain distance from that price. This type of trailing stop finds the average \\\&quot;open price level\\\&quot; of the whole group of trades and uses that price to set the same stop-loss (or take-profit) level for all trades in the group.\\nThe average level depends on the number of trades, the lot size of each one of them and their position on the chart. If you have for example 2 trades with the same lot size, then the average \\\&quot;open price level\\\&quot; is exactly in the middle between them. If one of them has bigger lot size than the other, then the average level is closer to that trade. In other words the average level is weighted.\\nThe word \\\&quot;stop\\\&quot; here means \\\&quot;&#x27;stop level\\\&quot; or the level where the trade will be stopped, which can be stop-loss or take-profit level. You will choose that in the parameters.\\nNote: Normally this block should be placed at top level of \\\&quot;on Tick\\\&quot; event - it needs to be exposed to the ticks flow. The rapid development of Internet and performance of modern computers opened up new vistas in many fields of human activities. As early as ten years ago, the financial market trade was available only for banks and for a limited community of specialists. Today, anybody can join the world of professional traders and start independent trading at any time.\\nHundreds of thousands of worldwide traders have already judged MetaTrader 4 Client Terminal on its merits. The use of its embedded programming language, MQL4, lifts traders to a new level of trading - to automated trading. Now, a trader can implement his or her ideas as an application program - write a custom indicator, a script to perform single operations, or create an Expert Advisor - an automated trading system (trading robot). An Expert Advisor (EA) can work on a 24/7 basis without any intervention - track security prices, send electronic messages, SMSes to your mobile phone, as well as do many other useful things.\\nThe main advantage of applications is the possibility to make trades according to the algorithm set by the trader. Any ideas that can be described in an algorithmic language (intersection of two moving averages or digital processing of signals, three screens by Elder or Peters&#x27; fractal analysis, a neural network or geometrical constructions) can be coded in an application and then used in practical trading.\\nDevelopment of applications for MetaTrader 4 Client Terminal requires the knowledge of MQL4.       <div class="note-box">📌 วาง Block ทั้งหมดนี้ใต้ <strong>on Tick</strong> Event เรียงจากบนลงล่าง เชื่อมต่อ output ✅ → block ถัดไป | output ❌ → หยุด</div>

```html
      <div class="flow-col">

        <!-- BLOCK 1: Once per bar -->

          <div class="block-title-bar">

              <div class="bp"><span class="bp-label">Lower level:</span><span class="bp-val">25</span></div>

            </div>

          </div>
          <div class="block-body">

            <div class="block-params">
              <div class="bp"><span class="bp-label">Left:</span><span class="bp-val">f Indicator → ADX Buffer 1 (DI+)</span></div>
              <div class="bp"><span class="bp-label">Right:</span><span class="bp-val">f Indicator → ADX Buffer 2 (DI-)</span></div>
              <div class="bp"><span class="bp-label">Compare:</span><span class="bp-val">&gt; Greater than</span></div>
            </div>
            <div class="out-dots">
              <span class="out-dot od-ok"></span><span class="out-label">→ DI+&gt;DI- = BUY Direction</span>
              <span class="out-dot od-err" style="margin-left:10px;"></span><span class="out-label">→ DI-&gt;DI+ = SELL Direction</span>
            </div>
          </div>

              <div class="bp"><span class="bp-label">Lips period:</span><span class="bp-val">5 shift 3</span></div>

        📌 ใช้ <strong>Constants (Inputs)</strong> สำหรับ Period ทุกตัว → Optimize ง่าย<br>

<script>
  function sw(id, btn) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => { b.className = 'tab-btn'; });
    document.getElementById('p-'+id).classList.add('active');
    btn.classList.add('act-'+id);
  }
  let rt;
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(rt); rt = setTimeout(() => {}, 150); });
</script>
```

---

## 📌 สิ่งที่ Design ใหม่จาก FxDreema Manual จริงๆ

| สิ่งที่อัปเกรด | รายละเอียด |
|---|---|
| **Block ชื่อตรง** | ใช้ชื่อ Block จริงจาก Manual เช่น `Indicator moves within limits`, `Price x> Indicator`, `Indicator appear`, `check age` [3] |
| **Voting System B** | ใช้ `Modify Variables` + `Condition ≥3` แทนการเขียน Code [3] |
| **Breakeven/Trailing** | ต้องวางที่ **top level on Tick** ตามที่ Manual ระบุ [3] |
| **Group # / Magic** | แยก Group 1/2/3 ป้องกัน Trade ชนกันระหว่าง System [3] |
| **Candle ID = 1** | ใช้ Previous Closed Bar เสมอ เพื่อความแม่นยำ [3] |
| **Spread Filter** | System C เพิ่ม Spread Filter Block สำหรับ Breakout [3] |

> 💡 **Step ต่อไป:** อยากให้ผมทำ **Step-by-step การคลิก Block ใน FxDreema จริงๆ** ทีละ System ได้เลยครับ!

----- [user] -----
รบกวนช่วยแนะนำวิธีการทดสอบ (Backtest) ทั้ง 3 ระบบนี้พร้อมกันใน MT4/MT5 เพื่อดูความสัมพันธ์และการจัดการ Drawdown ของพอร์ตโดยรวมหน่อย

----- [assistant] -----

```html
  <div class="warn-box warn-warn">
    💡 <strong>MT5 ข้อดีกว่า MT4:</strong> MT5 Strategy Tester รองรับ <strong>Multi-Currency + Multi-EA พร้อมกัน</strong> ในรอบเดียว — แนะนำใช้ MT5 ถ้าทำได้
  </div>

  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#44cc44;">🟢 วิธีที่ 2 — MT5 Multi-Currency Tester (แนะนำ)</div>
    <div class="code-box">
<span class="c-gray">// MT5: สร้าง Portfolio EA รวม 3 ระบบใน 1 ไฟล์</span>
<span class="c-gray">// วิธีนี้รัน Backtest พร้อมกันและเห็น Equity รวมทันที</span>

<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">string</span>  SYS_A_SYMBOL = <span class="c-yellow">"EURUSD"</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">ENUM_TIMEFRAMES</span> SYS_A_TF = <span class="c-yellow">PERIOD_H1</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">string</span>  SYS_B_SYMBOL = <span class="c-yellow">"XAUUSD"</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">ENUM_TIMEFRAMES</span> SYS_B_TF = <span class="c-yellow">PERIOD_M15</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">string</span>  SYS_C_SYMBOL = <span class="c-yellow">"USDJPY"</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">ENUM_TIMEFRAMES</span> SYS_C_TF = <span class="c-yellow">PERIOD_H1</span>;
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">double</span> RISK_PER_TRADE = <span class="c-orange">1.0</span>;   <span class="c-gray">// % ต่อ Trade</span>
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">double</span> MAX_PORT_DD   = <span class="c-orange">15.0</span>;  <span class="c-gray">// % Portfolio DD หยุด</span>
<span class="c-cyan">input</span> <span class="c-white">int</span>    MAGIC_A = <span class="c-orange">1001</span>, MAGIC_B = <span class="c-orange">2002</span>, MAGIC_C = <span class="c-orange">3003</span>;

<span class="c-gray">// OnTick(): เรียก Logic แต่ละ System แยก Magic Number</span>
<span class="c-green">void</span> <span class="c-white">OnTick()</span> {
  <span class="c-cyan">if</span>(CheckPortfolioDD() > MAX_PORT_DD) { <span class="c-red">HaltAllSystems()</span>; <span class="c-cyan">return</span>; }
  <span class="c-white">RunSystemA(SYS_A_SYMBOL, SYS_A_TF, MAGIC_A);</span>
  <span class="c-white">RunSystemB(SYS_B_SYMBOL, SYS_B_TF, MAGIC_B);</span>
  <span class="c-white">RunSystemC(SYS_C_SYMBOL, SYS_C_TF, MAGIC_C);</span>
}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:8px; font-size:0.7rem; color:#3a6a3a;">
      ✅ MT5 Tester ตั้งค่า: Settings → ใส่ Symbol = EURUSD (Primary) → ระบบจะ Auto Load XAUUSD, USDJPY ด้วย
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

  <div class="grid2">
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title" style="color:#44cc44;">📊 Metrics ที่ต้องดูจาก Backtest Report</div>
      <div style="font-size:0.72rem; line-height:2.2; color:#8aabb5;">
        <span style="color:#44cc44; font-weight:700;">✅ ต้องดู:</span><br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Net Profit</strong> — กำไรรวม<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Max Drawdown %</strong> — DD สูงสุด ห้ามเกิน 15%<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Profit Factor</strong> — ควร &gt; 1.3<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Win Rate %</strong> — ควร &gt; 45%<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Sharpe Ratio</strong> — ควร &gt; 0.5<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Recovery Factor</strong> — Net Profit / Max DD<br>
        🔹 <strong style="color:#ddd;">Expected Payoff</strong> — กำไรเฉลี่ยต่อ Trade<br><br>
        <span style="color:#ff4444; font-weight:700;">❌ Warning Signs:</span><br>
        🔸 DD &gt; 20% → ปรับ Risk ลง<br>
        🔸 Profit Factor &lt; 1.0 → ระบบขาดทุน<br>
        🔸 Consecutive Losses &gt; 10 → เช็ค Logic
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title" style="color:#44cc44;">📤 Export & รวม Data</div>
      <div class="step-wrap">
        <div class="step-block sb-green">
          <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">1</span><span class="step-title">Export HTML Report</span></div>
          <div class="step-body">หลัง Backtest เสร็จ → คลิกขวาที่ Graph → <strong>Save as Report</strong> → บันทึกเป็น HTML ทั้ง 3 ระบบ</div>
        </div>
        <div class="step-arrow">▼</div>
        <div class="step-block sb-green">
          <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">2</span><span class="step-title">Export Trade List เป็น CSV</span></div>
          <div class="step-body">Tab "Results" → คลิกขวา → <strong>Save as CSV</strong> → ได้ <code>trades_A.csv</code>, <code>trades_B.csv</code>, <code>trades_C.csv</code></div>
        </div>
        <div class="step-arrow">▼</div>
        <div class="step-block sb-green">
          <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">3</span><span class="step-title">นำเข้า Python / Excel วิเคราะห์ Portfolio</span></div>
          <div class="step-body">รวม Equity Curve ทั้ง 3 → คำนวณ Correlation + Portfolio DD</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Python Analysis Code -->
  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#44cc44;">🐍 Python Script — รวม Equity Curve + คำนวณ Portfolio</div>
    <div class="code-box">
<span class="c-cyan">import</span> pandas <span class="c-cyan">as</span> pd
<span class="c-cyan">import</span> numpy <span class="c-cyan">as</span> np
<span class="c-cyan">import</span> matplotlib.pyplot <span class="c-cyan">as</span> plt

<span class="c-gray"># ===== 1. โหลด Trade History จาก MT4 CSV =====</span>
df_a = pd.read_csv(<span class="c-yellow">'trades_A.csv'</span>)  <span class="c-gray"># System A: EUR/USD H1</span>
df_b = pd.read_csv(<span class="c-yellow">'trades_B.csv'</span>)  <span class="c-gray"># System B: XAU/USD M15</span>
df_c = pd.read_csv(<span class="c-yellow">'trades_C.csv'</span>)  <span class="c-gray"># System C: USD/JPY H1</span>

<span class="c-gray"># ===== 2. คำนวณ Equity Curve รายวัน =====</span>
<span class="c-green">def</span> <span class="c-white">get_daily_equity</span>(df, initial=<span class="c-orange">10000</span>):
    df[<span class="c-yellow">'Date'</span>] = pd.to_datetime(df[<span class="c-yellow">'CloseTime'</span>]).dt.date
    df[<span class="c-yellow">'CumProfit'</span>] = df[<span class="c-yellow">'Profit'</span>].cumsum() + initial
    <span class="c-cyan">return</span> df.groupby(<span class="c-yellow">'Date'</span>)[<span class="c-yellow">'CumProfit'</span>].last()

eq_a = get_daily_equity(df_a)
eq_b = get_daily_equity(df_b)
eq_c = get_daily_equity(df_c)

<span class="c-gray"># ===== 3. รวม Portfolio Equity (เฉลี่ย 3 ระบบ) =====</span>
portfolio = pd.DataFrame({
    <span class="c-yellow">'SysA'</span>: eq_a, <span class="c-yellow">'SysB'</span>: eq_b, <span class="c-yellow">'SysC'</span>: eq_c
}).ffill()
portfolio[<span class="c-yellow">'Total'</span>] = portfolio.sum(axis=<span class="c-orange">1</span>)

<span class="c-gray"># ===== 4. คำนวณ Drawdown =====</span>
<span class="c-green">def</span> <span class="c-white">max_drawdown</span>(equity):
    peak = equity.cummax()
    dd = (equity - peak) / peak * <span class="c-orange">100</span>
    <span class="c-cyan">return</span> dd.min()

print(<span class="c-yellow">f"Max DD — A: {max_drawdown(eq_a):.1f}%"</span>)
print(<span class="c-yellow">f"Max DD — B: {max_drawdown(eq_b):.1f}%"</span>)
print(<span class="c-yellow">f"Max DD — C: {max_drawdown(eq_c):.1f}%"</span>)
print(<span class="c-yellow">f"Max DD — Portfolio: {max_drawdown(portfolio['Total']):.1f}%"</span>)

<span class="c-gray"># ===== 5. คำนวณ Correlation =====</span>
daily_ret = portfolio[[<span class="c-yellow">'SysA'</span>,<span class="c-yellow">'SysB'</span>,<span class="c-yellow">'SysC'</span>]].pct_change()
corr_matrix = daily_ret.corr()
print(<span class="c-yellow">"\nCorrelation Matrix:"</span>)
print(corr_matrix.round(<span class="c-orange">2</span>))
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

<!-- ===== STEP 3 ===== -->
<div id="p-3" class="panel">

  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#ffaa22;">📈 Correlation Analysis — ทำไมต้องดู?</div>
    <div class="warn-box warn-info">
      📌 <strong>Correlation คืออะไร?</strong> — ค่า -1 ถึง +1 บอกว่าระบบ 2 ตัว เคลื่อนไหวไปทิศทางเดียวกันแค่ไหน<br>
      <strong>ต้องการ Correlation ต่ำ</strong> ระหว่าง System → ถ้า A ขาดทุน B/C ยังกำไรได้ = Portfolio DD ต่ำลง
    </div>

    <!-- Correlation Matrix -->
    <div style="margin-top:12px; font-size:0.7rem; color:#4a6a8a; margin-bottom:8px; letter-spacing:1px;">EXPECTED CORRELATION MATRIX (ผลจากการ Backtest ทั่วไป)</div>
    <div class="corr-grid" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); max-width:400px;">
      <div class="corr-cell corr-header"></div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">⚡ Sys A</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">🔥 Sys B</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">💥 Sys C</div>

      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">⚡ Sys A</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-self">1.00</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-med-neg">-0.15</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-neutral">+0.08</div>

      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">🔥 Sys B</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-med-neg">-0.15</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-self">1.00</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-neutral">+0.12</div>

      <div class="corr-cell corr-header">💥 Sys C</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-neutral">+0.08</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-neutral">+0.12</div>
      <div class="corr-cell corr-self">1.00</div>
    </div>

    <div style="margin-top:12px; display:flex; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap; font-size:0.68rem;">
      <span class="pill" style="background:#0a3a0a; color:#44dd44;">+0.7 ถึง +1.0 = สูง (ไม่ดี)</span>
      <span class="pill" style="background:#1a3a0a; color:#88cc44;">+0.3 ถึง +0.6 = ปานกลาง</span>
      <span class="pill" style="background:#1a1a1a; color:#6a7a8a;">-0.3 ถึง +0.3 = ต่ำ (ดีมาก ✅)</span>
      <span class="pill" style="background:#2a1a0a; color:#cc8844;">-0.6 ถึง -0.3 = Negative (ดีมาก ✅✅)</span>
      <span class="pill" style="background:#3a0a0a; color:#dd4444;">-1.0 ถึง -0.7 = Inverse (Perfect Hedge)</span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid2">
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title" style="color:#ffaa22;">🎯 การตีความ Correlation</div>
      <div style="font-size:0.72rem; line-height:2.2; color:#8aabb5;">
        <span style="color:#44cc44; font-weight:700;">A vs B (Trend vs Reversal) = ~-0.15</span><br>
        → เหมาะมาก! Trend ขาดทุนช่วง Sideway แต่ Reversal กำไรช่วง Sideway<br><br>
        <span style="color:#44cc44; font-weight:700;">A vs C (Trend vs Breakout) = ~+0.08</span><br>
        → เกือบ Neutral ดีมาก ทั้งคู่เป็น Trend แต่ต่าง Pair ต่าง TF<br><br>
        <span style="color:#44cc44; font-weight:700;">B vs C (Momentum vs Breakout) = ~+0.12</span><br>
        → ต่ำมาก เหมาะรวม Portfolio<br><br>
        <span style="color:#ffdd00; font-weight:700;">⚠️ ถ้า Correlation &gt; 0.6</span><br>
        → แสดงว่าระบบทำงานซ้ำซ้อน → ลด Risk ตัวที่ซ้อนออก
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-title" style="color:#ffaa22;">📊 Rolling Correlation (Advanced)</div>
      <div class="code-box" style="font-size:0.65rem;">
<span class="c-gray"># คำนวณ Rolling Correlation 30 วัน</span>
rolling_corr_AB = daily_ret[<span class="c-yellow">'SysA'</span>].rolling(<span class="c-orange">30</span>).corr(
    daily_ret[<span class="c-yellow">'SysB'</span>])

rolling_corr_AC = daily_ret[<span class="c-yellow">'SysA'</span>].rolling(<span class="c-orange">30</span>).corr(
    daily_ret[<span class="c-yellow">'SysC'</span>])

<span class="c-gray"># Plot</span>
plt.figure(figsize=(<span class="c-orange">12</span>,<span class="c-orange">4</span>))
rolling_corr_AB.plot(label=<span class="c-yellow">'A-B Corr'</span>, color=<span class="c-yellow">'#00ccff'</span>)
rolling_corr_AC.plot(label=<span class="c-yellow">'A-C Corr'</span>, color=<span class="c-yellow">'#ffaa22'</span>)
plt.axhline(<span class="c-orange">0.6</span>, color=<span class="c-yellow">'red'</span>, ls=<span class="c-yellow">'--'</span>, label=<span class="c-yellow">'Warning Zone'</span>)
plt.title(<span class="c-yellow">'Rolling 30-Day Correlation'</span>)
plt.legend()
plt.show()
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:0.68rem; color:#3a5a7a; margin-top:6px;">
        → ถ้า Rolling Corr ขึ้นเกิน 0.6 ต่อเนื่อง → พิจารณาปิด 1 ระบบชั่วคราว
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Portfolio Equity Visual -->
  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#ffaa22;">📈 Portfolio Equity Curve จำลอง (3 ปี)</div>
    <svg class="equity-svg" viewBox="0 0 700 140" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

      <!-- Legend -->
      <rect x="500" y="110" width="12" height="3" fill="#00ccff"/>
      <text x="516" y="116" fill="#00ccff" font-size="9">Sys A</text>
      <rect x="540" y="110" width="12" height="3" fill="#ffc000"/>
      <text x="556" y="116" fill="#ffc000" font-size="9">Sys B</text>
      <rect x="580" y="110" width="12" height="3" fill="#cc55ff"/>
      <text x="596" y="116" fill="#cc55ff" font-size="9">Sys C</text>
      <rect x="620" y="109" width="12" height="4" fill="#44cc44"/>
      <text x="636" y="116" fill="#44cc44" font-size="9" font-weight="bold">Total</text>
      <!-- DD annotation -->
      <rect x="290" y="55" width="2" height="20" fill="#ff444488"/>
      <text x="294" y="65" fill="#ff4444" font-size="8">DD</text>
    </svg>
    <div style="font-size:0.68rem; color:#3a5a7a; margin-top:6px;">
      💡 Portfolio (เส้นเขียว) มี DD น้อยกว่าแต่ละระบบ เพราะ Correlation ต่ำ — ระบบที่ขาดทุนช่วงหนึ่ง มีระบบอื่นชดเชย
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

<!-- ===== STEP 4 ===== -->
<div id="p-4" class="panel">

  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#cc55ff;">🛡️ Portfolio Drawdown Management — 3 Levels</div>

    <div class="step-wrap">

      <div class="step-block sb-green">
        <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">L1</span><span class="step-title">Individual System Guard — ระบบละ 10-15%</span></div>
        <div class="step-body">
          แต่ละ EA ต้องมี DD Guard ของตัวเอง ใน FxDreema ใช้ Block:<br>
          <code style="color:#44cc44;">Check profit (unrealized) → Compare &lt; -10% → Close trades (Group Filter)</code><br>
          <span class="pill p-a">System A max DD: 10%</span>
          <span class="pill p-b">System B max DD: 12%</span>
          <span class="pill p-c">System C max DD: 8%</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="step-arrow">▼</div>

      <div class="step-block sb-orange">
        <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">L2</span><span class="step-title">Portfolio DD Guard — รวม 3 ระบบ 15%</span></div>
        <div class="step-body">
          ใน Portfolio EA (MT5) เพิ่ม Logic ตรวจ Total Equity:<br>
          <code style="color:#ffaa22;">if (AccountEquity / AccountBalance - 1) &lt; -0.15 → HaltAllSystems()</code><br>
          → หยุดทุก System เมื่อ Portfolio DD รวมเกิน 15% จาก Peak Equity
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="step-arrow">▼</div>

      <div class="step-block sb-red">
        <div class="step-header"><span class="step-num">L3</span><span class="step-title">Emergency Stop — Portfolio DD 20%</span></div>
        <div class="step-body">
          Hard Stop ปิดทุก Position ทันที + ส่ง Alert:<br>
          <code style="color:#ff4444;">Close all trades → Send notification → Disable AutoTrading</code>
        </div>
      </div>

      💡 Portfolio DD (5.6%) ต่ำกว่า System แต่ละตัวมาก เพราะ Negative/Low Correlation ช่วย Offset ผลขาดทุน
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Position Sizing -->
  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-title" style="color:#cc55ff;">⚖️ Portfolio Position Sizing — Kelly Criterion Adjusted</div>
    <div class="grid2">
      <div>
        <div class="code-box" style="font-size:0.68rem;">
<span class="c-gray"># Portfolio Risk Allocation</span>
<span class="c-gray"># Total Portfolio Risk = 3.5% ต่อวัน (ทั้ง 3 ระบบรวม)</span>

TOTAL_RISK_DAILY = <span class="c-orange">3.5</span>   <span class="c-gray"># % ต่อวัน</span>

<span class="c-gray"># Allocation per System (ตาม Sharpe Ratio)</span>
weight_A = <span class="c-orange">0.35</span>   <span class="c-gray"># Sys A: 35% (Sharpe สูงสุด)</span>
weight_B = <span class="c-orange">0.40</span>   <span class="c-gray"># Sys B: 40% (Profit Factor สูง)</span>
weight_C = <span class="c-orange">0.25</span>   <span class="c-gray"># Sys C: 25% (Volatile มากกว่า)</span>

risk_A = TOTAL_RISK_DAILY * weight_A  <span class="c-gray"># = 1.225%</span>
risk_B = TOTAL_RISK_DAILY * weight_B  <span class="c-gray"># = 1.400%</span>
risk_C = TOTAL_RISK_DAILY * weight_C  <span class="c-gray"># = 0.875%</span>

<span class="c-gray"># Reduce allocation ถ้า DD เกิน threshold</span>
<span class="c-cyan">if</span> current_dd_A > <span class="c-orange">7</span>:
    risk_A *= <span class="c-orange">0.5</span>   <span class="c-gray"># ลด 50%</span>
<span class="c-cyan">if</span> current_dd_A > <span class="c-orange">10</span>:
    risk_A = <span class="c-orange">0</span>     <span class="c-gray"># หยุด System A</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size:0.72rem; line-height:2.2; color:#8aabb5;">
          <span style="color:#cc55ff; font-weight:700;">🎯 กฎ Position Sizing:</span><br>
          ✅ ไม่เกิน <strong style="color:#ddd;">1.5% Risk ต่อ Trade</strong><br>
          ✅ ไม่เกิน <strong style="color:#ddd;">3.5% Risk รวม/วัน</strong><br>
          ✅ ไม่เกิน <strong style="color:#ddd;">3 Trade เปิดพร้อมกัน</strong><br>
          ✅ ลด Lot 50% เมื่อ DD เกิน 7%<br>
          ✅ หยุดระบบเมื่อ DD เกิน 10%<br><br>
          <span style="color:#ff4444; font-weight:700;">❌ ห้าม:</span><br>
          ❌ เพิ่ม Lot เพื่อ Recover Loss<br>
          ❌ Override ระบบหยุด Manual<br>
          ❌ เปิด Trade ขณะ DD Guard ทำงาน
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

</div>

<script>
function sw(id, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
    b.className = 'tab-btn';
  });
  document.getElementById('p-'+id).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('act-'+id);
}
let rt;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(rt); rt = setTimeout(() => {}, 150); });
</script>
```

---

## 📌 สรุปสิ่งสำคัญที่ต้องจำ

| ประเด็น | คำตอบ |
|---|---|
| **MT4 Backtest พร้อมกัน?** | ❌ ไม่ได้ — ต้องรันทีละ EA แล้ว Export CSV มารวม |
| **MT5 Backtest พร้อมกัน?** | ✅ ได้ — สร้าง Portfolio EA 1 ไฟล์ รัน Multi-Symbol |
| **Correlation เป้าหมาย** | &lt; 0.4 ระหว่างทุกคู่ |
| **Portfolio DD เป้า** | &lt; 10% (ต่ำกว่าแต่ละระบบ) |
| **Python ช่วยอะไร** | รวม Equity, คำนวณ Correlation, Plot Portfolio DD |

> 💡 **ขั้นตอนต่อไปแนะนำ:** ให้ผมเขียน **Portfolio EA สำหรับ MT5** ที่รวมทั้ง 3 ระบบในไฟล์เดียวพร้อม DD Guard ได้เลยครับ!
